# GM Commands

GM commands can be entered in two ways:

- **In the world console window.** The leading dot (`.`) is not mandatory there, but you can use it.
- **In game, in the chat of the WoW client.** There every command must start with a dot, for example `.gm on`.

Some commands only work on a selected player or creature. Those cannot be used from the world console: see the *Console* column.

Some commands need a higher security level. To give an account a security level, use the `account set gmlevel` command from the console.

## Security levels

| Security | Name | Who can use the command |
| :------: | :--- | :---------------------- |
| 0 | SEC_PLAYER | Every account. |
| 1 | SEC_MODERATOR | Moderators. |
| 2 | SEC_GAMEMASTER | Game Masters. |
| 3 | SEC_ADMINISTRATOR | Administrators. |
| 4 | SEC_CONSOLE | The server console only. Do not give this security level to an account. |

The names are the ones of the `AccountTypes` enum in [Common.h](https://github.com/azerothcore/azerothcore-wotlk/blob/master/src/common/Common.h). An account can use every command of its own level and of the levels below it.

## Reading the tables

- **Command**: the name of the command, without the leading dot.
- **RBAC**: the role that includes the command by default, linked to its list in [rbac_linked_permissions](rbac_linked_permissions): 196 Administrator Commands, 197 Gamemaster Commands, 198 Moderator Commands, 199 Player Commands (or 192 to 195, the security-level roles, for the few permissions those hold directly). It is empty for a command that only groups subcommands.
- **Security**: the lowest security level that can use the command. This is the value the core applies when it differs from the role. The lists of one security level leave this column out.
- **Console**: *Yes* when the command also works from the world console, *No* when it only works in game.
- **Syntax**: how to type the command.
- **Description**: what the command does.

In the syntax:

| Notation | Meaning |
| :------- | :------ |
| `$name` | A text value, such as a player or account name. |
| `#value` | A number, such as an id or an amount. |
| `[...]` | Optional: the command also works without it. |
| `<a/b>` or `a/b` | Pick one of the listed words. |
| `Shift-click` | Some values can be given as a link: shift-click an item, spell or quest in game to add it to the chat line. |

Type `.help $command` in game or in the console to see the help text of a command.

## Commands

Every list is in alphabetical order. *All commands* has every command; the other lists have the same commands split by security level.

| <span id="worldstate-sunsreach-gatecounter">worldstate sunsreach gatecounter</span>           | [197](rbac_linked_permissions#role-197) | 3        | Yes     | `.worldstate sunsreach gatecounter <index> <value>`                                                 | Sets a Sunwell gate progression counter and displays current Suns Reach gate status. |
| <span id="worldstate-sunsreach-phase">worldstate sunsreach phase</span>                       | [197](rbac_linked_permissions#role-197) | 3        | Yes     | `.worldstate sunsreach phase <value>`                                                               | Sets the phase of Sun's Reach.<br>Valid values are:<br>0: Staging Area<br>1: Sanctum<br>2: Armory<br>3: Harbor. |
